## Findings

### deferred23-immediate-completion-source-inventory: Immediate completion verifier misses a newly added source owner

minor / resolved / confirmed; issue key: mim-deferred23-immediate-completion-source-inventory.

The frozen original writer returns verified-complete after a private post-publication insertion of one hidden YAML owner. Existing captured bytes and the native receipt chain still match, but source membership changes from 2956 to 2957. The later CLI verification reruns preparation and can catch drift; that does not support the earlier immediate completion claim. No actual maintained publication occurred in this reproduction. The current successor is now fixed and validated as described in disposition_reason.

Disposition: Writer v2 freshly enumerates source, generation, label, producer, absent-input and receipt memberships before and after completion verification; actual exact5-output publication and immediate verification passed. Original late-owner counterexample rejects;42 private controls pass with34 expected refusals.

### deferred23-publication-log-proof-role-alias: Typed suppression context accepts publication-log reuse of another proof role

minor / resolved / confirmed; issue key: mim-deferred23-publication-log-proof-role-alias.

The frozen verifier accepts a manifest whose publication_log points to the plan or canonical SSSOM, after the manifest is repinned. Both independent real native-chain fixtures pass. The completed publication log has a separate attribution role in the bounded contract. Current writer output uses the proper distinct log; this is an accepted-boundary defect, not observed corrupted mapping data or added scientific approval. The current successor is now fixed and validated as described in disposition_reason.

Disposition: The context and owned-context CLI reject role/path/inode reuse, including caller-only prior companion and loaded inputs, before publication and after reservation. Original independent plan/canonical/alias counterexamples reject and valid native refresh passes.

### deferred23-native-evidence-continuation-refusal: Owned-name context rejects expected receipt continuation for untouched rows

minor / resolved / confirmed; issue key: mim-deferred23-native-evidence-continuation-refusal.

After the exact 23-cell publication, the native assembler preserves all unselected mapping decisions and payloads but adds receipt26 continuation to six existing form-review entries at positions926,927,2610,2611,3013,3014. Row927 also adds the same receipt path/hash to superseded_scoped_review.receipt_inputs. These deterministic additions are required by existing native producers. The new typed context rejects them through raw equality for unrelated evidence, stopping companion refresh before any new companion or selector publication. The current successor is now fixed and validated as described in disposition_reason.

Disposition: The context reconstructs the existing authority/checkpoint and complete prior continuation from native replay, then permits only the exact new receipt suffix and existing supersession-map extension. All6 actual rows succeed;119 adversarial mutations reject; actual complete companion retry and dependent exports pass.

### deferred23-fixture-discards-history-prefix: Deferred-alias fixture overwrites an existing suppression-history prefix

minor / resolved / confirmed; issue key: mim-deferred23-fixture-history-prefix.

The original test fixture copies the live policy manifest and assigns a singleton owned_name_suppression_updates list. Once receipt26 adds a real history entry, the fixture discards that prefix. The production append-only-history guard correctly rejects the fabricated transition, causing the previously unchanged positive test to fail. This is a fixture defect, not a reason to change the production guard. The current successor is now fixed and validated as described in disposition_reason.

Disposition: The fixture retains every prior manifest update before appending one synthetic suffix. Independent empty/populated cases pass; deleted/altered-prefix cases remain rejected. All existing test functions/assertions remain unchanged apart from this fixture construction;120 focused tests and full suite pass.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- Source is working_tree in a real isolated clone whose actual HEAD is durable merged0ee56c11; exact candidate bytes and immutable evidence were copied and captured by native inspect. No source revision or supervisor binding is invented.
- The candidate was reviewed while generated data/evidence were still uncommitted on implementation HEAD01bf76b566d1504358e2352e9f0e3094bc3cdb43. A subsequent ordinary commit requires exact-content attestation, then actual all-green CI/queue and squash-content verification before merge approval.
- Native B4 was skipped for the absent nested default ontology-transform path. Actual external transforms elsewhere are not claimed absent. Other native rules pass; independent id-label CI remains a separate required publication gate.
- The single local suite skip is reported distinctly. Its module is unchanged; no newly introduced skip or waived assertion is inferred. No claim is made about future CI test counts.
- Historical failed attempts and original findings are retained, including the safely refused concurrent-code probe and the corrected reviewer-harness manifest expectation. They are not erased or relabelled as successful runs.
- Publisher verification applies to its captured publication phase; later authorized context/metadata changes do not justify rerunning that already completed one-time mapping transaction.
- This process/provenance review supplies no paid-provider research, role approval, identity merge, scientific-release approval, cycle advance or session restart.

findings:
- finding_id: deferred23-immediate-completion-source-inventory
  issue_key: mim-deferred23-immediate-completion-source-inventory
  category: provenance
  severity: minor
  native_severity: P3 / bounded pre-publication verification gap
  normalization_reason: Confirmed completion/provenance verification defect found
    before real publication; no demonstrated wrong identity, scientific approval or
    maintained data corruption.
  status: resolved
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Immediate completion verifier misses a newly added source owner
  description: The frozen original writer returns verified-complete after a private
    post-publication insertion of one hidden YAML owner. Existing captured bytes and
    the native receipt chain still match, but source membership changes from 2956
    to 2957. The later CLI verification reruns preparation and can catch drift; that
    does not support the earlier immediate completion claim. No actual maintained
    publication occurred in this reproduction. The current successor is now fixed
    and validated as described in disposition_reason.
  target_ids:
  - next23-writer-v1
  - next23-writer-membership-probe
  - next23-writer-membership-result
  field_paths:
  - promote_deferred_aliases_v1.py:verify_completed
  evidence_ids:

  - captured-001
  - captured-002
  - captured-003
  - executed-writer-v2

  - captured-083
  owner_paths:

    path: reports/owned_name_deferred_669_20261103/review-findings/completion-inventory-v1/operator-v1.py
    role: Run-owned external promote_deferred_aliases publisher; implement a versioned
      successor and preserve this original archive
  external_issues:
  - https://github.com/CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech/issues/1131
  disposition_reason: Writer v2 freshly enumerates source, generation, label, producer,
    absent-input and receipt memberships before and after completion verification;
    actual exact5-output publication and immediate verification passed. Original late-owner
    counterexample rejects;42 private controls pass with34 expected refusals.
  previous_occurrences:

    review_id: 20261104T045618Z-deferred23-independent-publication-proof-findings
    finding_id: deferred23-immediate-completion-source-inventory
- finding_id: deferred23-publication-log-proof-role-alias
  issue_key: mim-deferred23-publication-log-proof-role-alias
  category: provenance
  severity: minor
  native_severity: P3 / bounded pre-publication verification gap
  normalization_reason: Confirmed completion/provenance verification defect found
    before real publication; no demonstrated wrong identity, scientific approval or
    maintained data corruption.
  status: resolved
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Typed suppression context accepts publication-log reuse of another proof
    role
  description: The frozen verifier accepts a manifest whose publication_log points
    to the plan or canonical SSSOM, after the manifest is repinned. Both independent
    real native-chain fixtures pass. The completed publication log has a separate
    attribution role in the bounded contract. Current writer output uses the proper
    distinct log; this is an accepted-boundary defect, not observed corrupted mapping
    data or added scientific approval. The current successor is now fixed and validated
    as described in disposition_reason.
  target_ids:
  - next23-context-v1
  - next23-context-alias-probe
  - next23-context-alias-result
  field_paths:

    role: src/mediaingredientmech/owned_name_suppression_refresh.py
  external_issues:

  disposition_reason: The context and owned-context CLI reject role/path/inode reuse,
    including caller-only prior companion and loaded inputs, before publication and
    after reservation. Original independent plan/canonical/alias counterexamples reject
    and valid native refresh passes.
  previous_occurrences:

- finding_id: deferred23-native-evidence-continuation-refusal
  issue_key: mim-deferred23-native-evidence-continuation-refusal
  category: consistency
  severity: minor
  native_severity: P3 / fail-closed companion refresh blocker
  normalization_reason: The new context safely refuses valid deterministic provenance
    additions; no wrong mapping or strengthened scientific disposition is demonstrated.
  status: resolved
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Owned-name context rejects expected receipt continuation for untouched rows
  description: After the exact 23-cell publication, the native assembler preserves
    all unselected mapping decisions and payloads but adds receipt26 continuation
    to six existing form-review entries at positions926,927,2610,2611,3013,3014. Row927
    also adds the same receipt path/hash to superseded_scoped_review.receipt_inputs.
    These deterministic additions are required by existing native producers. The new
    typed context rejects them through raw equality for unrelated evidence, stopping
    companion refresh before any new companion or selector publication. The current
    successor is now fixed and validated as described in disposition_reason.
  target_ids:
  - next23-context-before-continuation-fix
  - next23-native-refresh-continuation-failure
  - next23-independent-continuation-finding
  field_paths:
  - 'verify_owned_name_suppression: unrelated base evidence equality'
  - basis.form_followup.continuation
  - basis.superseded_scoped_review.receipt_inputs
  evidence_ids:
  - captured-119
  - captured-007
  - captured-008
  - captured-009
  - captured-084
  - captured-087
  - independent-1133-reprobe
  - independent-derived-audit
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech
    path: src/mediaingredientmech/owned_name_suppression_refresh.py
    role: Bounded typed context must independently prove expected native extensions
      while preserving all other evidence and scientific decisions
  external_issues:
  - https://github.com/CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech/issues/1133
  disposition_reason: The context reconstructs the existing authority/checkpoint and
    complete prior continuation from native replay, then permits only the exact new
    receipt suffix and existing supersession-map extension. All6 actual rows succeed;119
    adversarial mutations reject; actual complete companion retry and dependent exports
    pass.
  previous_occurrences:

    review_id: 20261104T051118Z-deferred23-independent-native-continuation-finding
    finding_id: deferred23-native-evidence-continuation-refusal
- finding_id: deferred23-fixture-discards-history-prefix
  issue_key: mim-deferred23-fixture-history-prefix
  category: consistency
  severity: minor
  native_severity: P3 / fixture-only validation blocker
  normalization_reason: The fixture constructs an invalid transition and production
    rejects it safely; no maintained mapping or scientific defect is demonstrated.
  status: resolved
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Deferred-alias fixture overwrites an existing suppression-history prefix
  description: The original test fixture copies the live policy manifest and assigns
    a singleton owned_name_suppression_updates list. Once receipt26 adds a real history
    entry, the fixture discards that prefix. The production append-only-history guard
    correctly rejects the fabricated transition, causing the previously unchanged
    positive test to fail. This is a fixture defect, not a reason to change the production
    guard. The current successor is now fixed and validated as described in disposition_reason.
  target_ids:
  - next23-prefix-fixture-before
  - next23-prefix-fixture-failure
  field_paths:
  - tests/test_owned_name_suppression_refresh.py:build_case
  - manifest[owned_name_suppression_updates]
  evidence_ids:
  - captured-120
  - captured-010
  - captured-011
  - captured-087
  - independent-1134-reprobe
  - next23-full-suite-v1
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech
    path: tests/test_owned_name_suppression_refresh.py
    role: Fix fixture construction only; retain all production append-only prefix
      checks and pre-existing test assertions
  external_issues:
  - https://github.com/CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech/issues/1134
  disposition_reason: The fixture retains every prior manifest update before appending
    one synthetic suffix. Independent empty/populated cases pass; deleted/altered-prefix
    cases remain rejected. All existing test functions/assertions remain unchanged
    apart from this fixture construction;120 focused tests and full suite pass.
  previous_occurrences:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech
    review_id: 20261104T052359Z-deferred23-independent-fixture-prefix-finding
    finding_id: deferred23-fixture-discards-history-prefix
